\begin{split}I(F,U_{1},U_{2})&\leq C\int_{(\Lambda^{2})^{|S|}}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{S}}\prod_{i,j\in S:i<j}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{A}_{ij}}\frac{|x_{i_{0}}-y_{i_{0}}|^{2}}{\tau_{i_{0}}^{2}}\prod_{i\in V_{X}}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(x_{i}-y_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{|x_{i}-y_{i}|\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}x_{i}\mathrm{d}y_{i}\\
&\leq C\int_{(\Lambda^{2})^{|S|}}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{S}}\prod_{i,j\in S:i<j}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{A}_{ij}}\frac{\max_{i\in S}|x_{i}-y_{i}|^{2}}{\tau_{i_{0}}^{2}}\prod_{i\in V_{X}}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(x_{i}-y_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{|x_{i}-y_{i}|\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}x_{i}\mathrm{d}y_{i}.\end{split}

Suppose that
U_{2}\neq\emptyset
and let
i_{0}\in U_{2}
. Then, there exists a constant
C>0
depending on
\beta
and
|S|
such that

(A.37)

I(F,U_{1},U_{2})\leq C\int_{(\Lambda^{2})^{|S|}}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{S}}\prod_{i,j\in S:i<j}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{A}_{ij}}\mathbf{1}_{i_{0}\notin I_{\mathrm{good}}^{Z}}\prod_{i\in V_{X}}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(x_{i}-y_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{|x_{i}-y_{i}|\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}x_{i}\mathrm{d}y_{i}.

Suppose that
F\neq\emptyset
and let
i_{0}j_{0}\in F
.

(A.38)

|e^{\beta(v_{i_{0}j_{0}}-w_{i_{0}j_{0}})}-1|\leq C|v_{i_{0}j_{0}}-w_{i_{0}j_{0}}|.

By Lemma (
3.49
) or (
3.54
), we can write that

(A.39)

|w_{ij}-v_{ij}|\leq C\frac{r_{i_{0}}r_{j_{0}}}{\min(\tau_{i_{0}},\tau_{j_{0}})^{2}}.

We conclude from (
A.35
)—(
A.39
) and
\tau_{i}\geq\frac{r_{i}}{4}
that there exists a constant
C>0
depending on
\beta
and
|S|
such that

(A.40)

|I_{1}^{w,\tau,L,\varepsilon_{0},Z}(S)-I_{1}^{v,\infty,0,\varepsilon_{0},\emptyset}(S)|\leq C\max_{i_{0}\in S}\int_{(\Lambda^{2})^{|S|}}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{S}}\prod_{i,j\in S:i<j}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{A}_{ij}}\left(\frac{|x_{i_{0}}-y_{i_{0}}|^{2}}{\tau_{i_{0}}^{2}}+\mathbf{1}_{i_{0}\notin I_{\mathrm{good}}^{Z}}\right)\\
\times\prod_{i\in V_{X}}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(x_{i}-y_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{|x_{i}-y_{i}|\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}x_{i}\mathrm{d}y_{i}.

On the other hand, there exists a constant
C>0
depending on
\beta
and
|S|
such that

(A.41)

|I_{1}^{v,\infty,0,\varepsilon_{0},\emptyset}(S)-I_{1}^{v,\infty,0,\infty,\emptyset}(S)|\leq C\max_{i_{0}\in S}\int_{(\Lambda^{2})^{|S|}}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{S}}\prod_{i\in V_{X}}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(x_{i}-y_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{|x_{i_{0}}-y_{i_{0}}|\geq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}.

Step 2: integration of (
A.40
)

We then split the integral in the right-hand side of (
A.40
) according to the label of the largest
r_{i}
,
i\in S
. Again by a union bound,
\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{S}}\leq\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}_{c}(S)}\prod_{ij\in T}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{ij}}
.
We deduce from this that there exists a constant
C>0
depending on
|S|
such that

(A.42)

\int_{(\Lambda^{2})^{|S|}}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{S}}\prod_{i,j\in S:i<j}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{A}_{ij}}\left(\frac{|x_{i_{0}}-y_{i_{0}}|^{2}}{\tau_{i_{0}}^{2}}+\mathbf{1}_{i_{0}\notin I_{\mathrm{good}}^{Z}}\right)\prod_{i\in V_{X}}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(x_{i}-y_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{|x_{i}-y_{i}|\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}x_{i}\mathrm{d}y_{i}\\
\leq C\max_{T\in\mathcal{T}_{c}(S)}\max_{\iota_{S}\in S}J(\iota_{S},T,i_{0}),

where

J(\iota_{S},T,i_{0})\coloneqq\int_{(\Lambda^{2})^{|S|}}\prod_{ij\in T}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{ij}}\prod_{i,j\in S:i<j}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{A}_{ij}}\left(\frac{|x_{i_{0}}-y_{i_{0}}|^{2}}{\tau_{i_{0}}^{2}}+\mathbf{1}_{i_{0}\notin I_{\mathrm{good}}^{Z}}\right)\\
\times\prod_{i\in V_{X}}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(x_{i}-y_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{|x_{i}-y_{i}|\leq|x_{\iota_{S}}-y_{\iota_{S}}|\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}x_{i}\mathrm{d}y_{i}.

Define

\begin{split}\mathcal{D}_{ij}^{1}&=\{(x_{q},y_{q})_{q\in S}:d_{ij}=|x_{i}-x_{j}|\},\\
\mathcal{D}_{ij}^{2}&=\{(x_{q},y_{q})_{q\in S}:d_{ij}=|x_{i}-y_{j}|\},\\
\mathcal{D}_{ij}^{3}&=\{(x_{q},y_{q})_{q\in S}:d_{ij}=|y_{i}-x_{j}|\},\\
\mathcal{D}_{ij}^{4}&=\{(x_{q},y_{q})_{q\in S}:d_{ij}=|y_{i}-y_{j}|\}.\end{split}

We have

(A.43)

J(\iota_{S},T,i_{0})\leq C\max_{T^{1},\ldots,T^{4}:T^{1}\sqcup\cdots\sqcup T^{4}=T}J(\iota_{S},(T^{l})_{1\leq l\leq 4},i_{0}),

where

J(\iota_{S},(T^{l})_{1\leq l\leq 4},i_{0})=\int_{(\Lambda^{2})^{|S|}}\left(\prod_{l=1}^{4}\prod_{ij\in T^{l}}\mathbf{1}_{|z_{ij}|\leq M\max(\min(r_{i},r_{j}),\lambda)}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{D}_{ij}^{l}}\right)\left(\frac{|x_{\iota_{S}}-y_{\iota_{S}}|^{2}}{\tau_{i_{0}}^{2}}+\mathbf{1}_{i_{0}\notin I_{\mathrm{good}}^{Z}}\right)\\
\times\prod_{i\in V_{X}}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(x_{i}-y_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{|x_{i}-y_{i}|\leq|x_{\iota_{S}}-y_{\iota_{S}}|\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}x_{i}\mathrm{d}y_{i}.

We then let

z_{ij}=\begin{cases}x_{i}-x_{j}&\text{if $ij\in T^{1}$}\\
x_{i}-y_{j}&\text{if $ij\in T^{2}$}\\
y_{i}-x_{j}&\text{if $ij\in T^{3}$}\\
y_{i}-y_{j}&\text{if $ij\in T^{4}$}\end{cases}

and perform as previously the change of variables

(x_{i},y_{i})_{i\in S}\mapsto(x_{i_{0}},(z_{ij})_{ij\in T},(\vec{r}_{i})_{i\in S}).

Integrating the variables
(z_{ij})_{ij\in T}
, there exists
C>0
depending on
\beta,M
and
|S|
such that

\displaystyle J(\iota_{S},(T_{S}^{l})_{1\leq l\leq 4},i_{0})

\displaystyle\leq C\int\frac{1}{\tau_{i_{0}}^{2}}\prod_{i\in S}\max(r_{i},\lambda)^{2}\prod_{i\in S}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(r_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{r_{i}\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}\vec{r}_{i}\mathrm{d}x_{i_{0}}

\displaystyle+C\int\mathbf{1}_{i_{0}\notin I_{\mathrm{good}}^{Z}}\prod_{i\in S:i\neq\iota_{S}}\max(r_{i},\lambda)^{2}\prod_{i\in S}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(r_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{r_{i}\leq r_{\iota_{S}}\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}\vec{r}_{i}\mathrm{d}x_{i_{0}}.

Let us bound the second integral. First, if
i_{0}\notin I_{\mathrm{good}}^{Z}
, then by definition (
5.45
),

(A.44)

x_{i_{0}}\in\bigcup_{j\in V_{\mathrm{bad}}}\Bigr(B(x_{j},2M\max(r_{i_{0}},\lambda))\cup B(y_{j},2M\max(r_{i_{0}},\lambda))\Bigr).

Therefore, integrating out
x_{i_{0}}
and using that
r_{i_{0}}\leq r_{\iota_{S}}
, we get that there exists
C>0
depending on
\beta
,
|S|
,
M
and
\varepsilon_{0}
such that

\displaystyle\int\mathbf{1}_{i_{0}\notin I_{\mathrm{good}}^{Z}}\prod_{i\in S:i\neq\iota_{S}}\max(r_{i},\lambda)^{2}\prod_{i\in S}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(r_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{r_{i}\leq r_{\iota_{S}}\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}\vec{r}_{i}\mathrm{d}x_{i_{0}}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq C|V_{\mathrm{bad}}|\int\prod_{i\in S}\max(r_{i},\lambda)^{2}\prod_{i\in S}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(r_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{r_{i}\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}\vec{r}_{i}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq C|V_{\mathrm{bad}}|\left(R_{\beta,\lambda}^{(4-\beta)|S|}\mathbf{1}_{\beta\in(2,4)}+|\log\lambda|^{|S|}\mathbf{1}_{\beta=4}+\lambda^{(4-\beta)|S|}\mathbf{1}_{\beta>4}\right).

Thus, recalling that
R_{\beta,\lambda}^{4-\beta}=\lambda^{2-\beta}
, there exists a constant
C>0
depending on
\beta,M
and
\varepsilon_{0}
such that

(A.45)

\int\mathbf{1}_{i_{0}\notin I_{\mathrm{good}}^{Z}}\prod_{i\in S:i\neq\iota_{S}}\max(r_{i},\lambda)^{2}\prod_{i\in S}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(r_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{r_{i}\leq r_{\iota_{S}}\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}\vec{r}_{i}\mathrm{d}x_{i_{0}}\leq CN\lambda^{(2-\beta)|S|}|V_{\mathrm{bad}}|.

Turning to the first integral, there exists a constant
C>0
depending on
\beta,|S|
and
\varepsilon_{0}
such that

(A.46)

\int\frac{1}{\tau_{i_{0}}^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{\tau_{i_{0}}\geq 8\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\prod_{i\in S}\max(r_{i},\lambda)^{2}\prod_{i\in S}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(r_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{r_{i}\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}\vec{r}_{i}\mathrm{d}x_{i_{0}}\leq CN\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}^{-2}\lambda^{(2-\beta)|S|}.

Moreover, there exists a constant
C>0
depending on
\beta,|S|
and
\varepsilon_{0}
such that

(A.47)

\int\frac{1}{\tau_{i_{0}}^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{\tau_{i_{0}}=\frac{r_{i_{0}}}{4}}\prod_{i\in S}\max(r_{i},\lambda)^{2}\prod_{i\in S}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(r_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{r_{i}\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}\vec{r}_{i}\mathrm{d}x_{i_{0}}\leq CN|V_{\mathrm{bad}}|\lambda^{(2-\beta)|S|}.

It remains to bound

\int\frac{1}{\tau_{i_{0}}^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{\tau_{i_{0}}<8\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathbf{1}_{r_{i_{0}}\leq\mathrm{dist}(\{x_{i_{0}},y_{i_{0}}\},Z)}\prod_{i\in S}\max(r_{i},\lambda)^{2}\prod_{i\in S}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(r_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{r_{i}\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}\vec{r}_{i}\mathrm{d}x_{i_{0}}\\
\leq\sum_{j\in V_{\mathrm{bad}}}\int\frac{\mathbf{1}_{r_{i_{0}}\leq d_{i_{0}j}\leq 8\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}}{d_{i_{0}j}^{2}}\prod_{i\in S}\max(r_{i},\lambda)^{2}\prod_{i\in S}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(r_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{r_{i}\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}\vec{r}_{i}\mathrm{d}x_{i_{0}}.

One can easily show that

\sum_{j\in V_{\mathrm{bad}}}\int\frac{\mathbf{1}_{r_{i_{0}}\leq d_{i_{0}j}\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}}{d_{i_{0}j}^{2}}\prod_{i\in S}\max(r_{i},\lambda)^{2}\prod_{i\in S}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(r_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{r_{i}\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}\vec{r}_{i}\mathrm{d}x_{i_{0}}\\
\leq C|V_{\mathrm{bad}}|\int\log\frac{8\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}{\min_{i\in V_{X}}r_{i}}\prod_{i\in S}\max(r_{i},\lambda)^{2}\prod_{i\in S}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(r_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{r_{i}\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}\vec{r}_{i}.

Therefore, there exists
C>0
depending on
\beta,|S|
and
\varepsilon_{0}
such that

(A.48)

\int\frac{1}{\tau_{i_{0}}^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{\tau_{i_{0}}<8\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\prod_{i\in S}\max(r_{i},\lambda)^{2}\prod_{i\in S}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(r_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{r_{i}\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}\vec{r}_{i}\mathrm{d}x_{i_{0}}\leq C(|V_{\mathrm{bad}}|+N\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}^{-2})\lambda^{(2-\beta)|S|}.

Therefore, assembling (
A.45
), (
A.46
) and (
A.48
), we get that there exists a constant
C>0
depending on
\beta
,
M
,
|S|
and
\varepsilon_{0}
such that

J(\iota_{S},(T_{S}^{l})_{1\leq l\leq 4},i_{0})\leq C(|V_{\mathrm{bad}}|+N\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}^{-2})\lambda^{(2-\beta)|S|}.

Combining this with (
A.40
), (
A.42
) and (
A.43
) shows that there exists a constant
C>0
depending on
\beta
,
M
,
|S|
and
\varepsilon_{0}
such that

(A.49)

|I_{1}^{w,\tau,L,\varepsilon_{0},Z}(S)-I_{1}^{v,\infty,0,\varepsilon_{0},\emptyset}(S)|\leq C(|V_{\mathrm{bad}}|+\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}^{-2})\lambda^{(2-\beta)|S|}.

Step 3: integration of (
A.41
)

Splitting the phase space as in Step 2 shows that

\displaystyle\int_{(\Lambda^{2})^{|S|}}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{S}}\prod_{i\in V_{X}}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(x_{i}-y_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{|x_{i_{0}}-y_{i_{0}}|\geq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq CN\max_{j_{0}\in S}\int\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{S}}\prod_{i\in S:i\neq j_{0}}\max(r_{i},\lambda)^{2}\mathbf{1}_{r_{j_{0}}\geq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\prod_{i\in V_{X}}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(r_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{r_{i}\leq r_{j_{0}}}\mathrm{d}\vec{r}_{i}.

Integrating the variables
r_{i}
for
i\neq j_{0}
yields

\int_{(\Lambda^{2})^{|S|}}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{S}}\prod_{i\in V_{X}}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(x_{i}-y_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{|x_{i_{0}}-y_{i_{0}}|\geq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\leq C\int\max(r,\lambda)^{(4-\beta)|S|-3}\mathbf{1}_{r\geq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}r.

Using Lemma
A.2
, we obtain that there exists
C>0
depending on
\beta
,
M
,
\varepsilon_{0}
, and
|S|
such that

(A.50)

\int_{(\Lambda^{2})^{|S|}}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{S}}\prod_{i\in V_{X}}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(x_{i}-y_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{|x_{i_{0}}-y_{i_{0}}|\geq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\leq C\lambda^{(2-\beta)k}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}^{-2}.

Therefore, by (
A.41
), there exists
C>0
depending on
\beta,M
,
\varepsilon_{0}
, and
|S|
such that

(A.51)

|I_{1}^{v,\infty,0,\varepsilon_{0},\emptyset}(S)-I_{1}^{v,\infty,0,\infty,\emptyset}(S)|\leq CN\lambda^{(2-\beta)k}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}^{-2}.

Assembling (
A.49
) and (
A.51
) proves the lemma.
∎

Lemma A.7
.

Let
\beta\in(2,\infty)
and
p(\beta)
be as in Definition
2.7
. Let
V_{\mathrm{bad}}\subset[N]
and
V_{\mathrm{good}}=[N]\backslash V_{\mathrm{bad}}
. Let
X
be a subpartition of
V_{\mathrm{good}}
such that
|V_{X}|\leq p(\beta)
. Set
k\coloneqq|V_{X}|
. Let
X_{1},\ldots,X_{n}\subset X
be disjoint,
E_{1}\in\mathsf{E}^{X_{1}},\ldots,E_{n}\in\mathsf{E}^{X_{n}}
and
F\in\mathsf{E}^{\mathrm{Coa}_{X}(X_{1},\ldots,X_{n})}
.

Let
(w,\tau)\in\{(\tilde{v},\tilde{\tau}),(v^{Z},\tau^{Z})\}
. Recall
I_{\mathrm{good}}^{Z}
from (
5.45
). Let
L\in\mathbb{R}
. Set

(A.52)

I_{2}^{w,\tau,L,\varepsilon_{0},Z}\coloneqq\int_{(\Lambda^{2})^{k}}\prod_{i\in V_{X}}\mathbf{1}_{i\in I_{\mathrm{good}}^{Z}}\prod_{ij\in\cup_{l}E_{l}}f_{ij}^{w}\prod_{ij\in F}(-\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{ij}})\prod_{ij\in\cup_{l=1}^{n}\mathcal{E}^{\mathrm{inter}}(X_{l})}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{ij}^{c}}\\
\times\left(\prod_{S\in X}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{S}}\prod_{ij\in S:i<j}e^{-\beta w_{ij}}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{A}_{ij}}\right)\prod_{i\in V_{X}}e^{L\frac{|x_{i}-y_{i}|^{2}}{\tau_{i}^{2}}}\prod_{i\in V_{X}}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(x_{i}-y_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{|x_{i}-y_{i}|\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}x_{i}\mathrm{d}y_{i}.

Then, there exists a constant
C>0
depending on
\beta
,
M
,
\varepsilon_{0}
and
k
such that

(A.53)

|I_{2}^{w,\tau,L,\varepsilon_{0},Z}-I_{2}^{v,\infty,0,\varepsilon_{0},\emptyset}|\leq C(|V_{\mathrm{bad}}|+N\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}^{-2})\lambda^{(2-\beta)k}

and

(A.54)

|I_{2}^{v,\infty,0,\varepsilon_{0},\emptyset}-I_{2}^{v,\infty,0,0,\emptyset}|\leq C(|V_{\mathrm{bad}}|+N\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}^{-2})\lambda^{(2-\beta)k}

Proof of Lemma
A.7
.

As in the beginning of the proof of Lemma
A.6
, we may assume that
\tau_{i}\geq\frac{r_{i}}{4}
.

Step 1: starting point

Splitting
f_{ij}^{w}
into
a_{ij}^{w}+b_{ij}^{w}
with
a_{ij}^{w}
and
b_{ij}^{w}
as in Definition
4.24
, and using the parity argument of Lemma
4.28
, we get

I_{2}^{w,\tau,L,\varepsilon_{0},Z}=\left(\prod_{l=1}^{n}\sum_{E_{1,l}\subset E_{l}:E_{1,l}\in\mathrm{Eul}^{X_{l}}}\right)J_{1}^{w,\tau,L,\varepsilon_{0},Z}((E_{1,l})),

where

J_{1}^{w,\tau,L,\varepsilon_{0},Z}((E_{1,l}))\coloneqq\int_{(\Lambda^{2})^{|V_{X}|}}\prod_{i\in V_{X}}\mathbf{1}_{i\in I_{\mathrm{good}}^{Z}}\prod_{ij\in\cup_{l}E_{1,l}}a_{ij}^{w}\prod_{ij\in\cup_{l}E_{l}\setminus E_{1,l}}b_{ij}^{w}\prod_{ij\in F}(-\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{ij}})\prod_{ij\in\cup_{l}\mathcal{E}^{\mathrm{inter}}(X_{l})}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{ij}^{c}}\\
\times\left(\prod_{S\in X}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{S}}\prod_{ij\in S:i<j}e^{-\beta w_{ij}}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{A}_{ij}}\right)\prod_{i\in V_{X}}e^{L\frac{|x_{i}-y_{i}|^{2}}{\tau_{i}^{2}}}\prod_{i\in V_{X}}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(x_{i}-y_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{|x_{i}-y_{i}|\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}x_{i}\mathrm{d}y_{i}.

For every
l\in[n]
, fix
E_{1,l}\subset E_{l}
such that
E_{1,l}\in\mathrm{Eul}^{X_{l}}
. Set
E^{\mathrm{odd}}\coloneqq\cup_{l}E_{1,l}
and
E^{\mathrm{even}}\coloneqq E\setminus E^{\mathrm{odd}}
. Decomposing
a_{ij}^{w}=a_{ij}^{v}+(a_{ij}^{w}-a_{ij}^{v})
,
e^{-\beta w_{ij}}=e^{-\beta v_{ij}}(e^{\beta(v_{ij}-w_{ij})}-1)
, etc, and expanding, we may write

J_{1}^{w,\tau,L,\varepsilon_{0},Z}((E_{1,l}))=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}F_{1}\subset E^{\mathrm{odd}},F_{2}\subset E^{\mathrm{even}}\\
F_{3}\subset\mathcal{E}^{\mathrm{intra}}(X)\\
U_{1}\subset V_{X},U_{2}\subset V_{X}\end{subarray}}J_{2}(F_{1},F_{2},F_{3},U_{1},U_{2}),

where

J_{2}(F_{1},F_{2},F_{3},U_{1},U_{2})=\int_{(\Lambda^{2})^{|V_{X}|}}\prod_{ij\in F_{1}}(a_{ij}^{w}-a_{ij}^{v})\prod_{ij\in E^{\mathrm{odd}}\setminus F_{1}}a_{ij}^{v}\prod_{ij\in F_{2}}(b_{ij}^{w}-b_{ij}^{v})\prod_{ij\in E^{\mathrm{even}}\setminus F_{2}}b_{ij}^{v}\\
\prod_{S\in X}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{S}}\prod_{ij\in\mathcal{E}^{\mathrm{intra}}(X)}e^{-\beta v_{ij}}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{A}_{ij}}\prod_{ij\in F_{3}}(e^{\beta(v_{ij}-w_{ij})}-1)\prod_{ij\in\cup_{l}\mathcal{E}^{\mathrm{inter}}(X_{l})}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{ij}}\prod_{ij\in F}(-\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{ij}})\\
\times\prod_{ij\in U_{1}}\Bigr(e^{L\frac{|x_{i}-y_{i}|^{2}}{\tau_{i}^{2}}}-1\Bigr)\prod_{i\in U_{2}}(-1)\mathbf{1}_{i\notin I_{\mathrm{good}}^{Z}}.

Thus,

(A.55)

J_{1}^{w,\tau,L,\varepsilon_{0},Z}((E_{1,l}))-J_{1}^{v,\infty,0,\infty,\emptyset}((E_{1,l}))=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(F_{1},F_{2},F_{3},U_{1},U_{2})\\
\neq(\emptyset,\emptyset,\emptyset,\emptyset,\emptyset)\end{subarray}}J_{2}(F_{1},F_{2},F_{3},U_{1},U_{2}).

On the other hand,

(A.56)

|I_{2}^{v,\infty,0,\varepsilon_{0},\emptyset}((E_{1,l}))-I_{2}^{v,\infty,0,\infty,\emptyset}((E_{1,l}))|=\sum_{\emptyset\neq U_{0}\subset V_{X}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}E^{\mathrm{odd}}\subset E:\\
E^{\mathrm{odd}}\in\mathrm{Eul}^{X}\end{subarray}}J(E^{\mathrm{odd}},U_{0}),

where

(A.57)

J(E^{\mathrm{odd}},U_{0})\coloneqq\int_{(\Lambda^{2})^{|V_{X}|}}\prod_{ij\in E^{\mathrm{odd}}}a_{ij}^{w}\prod_{ij\in E\setminus E^{\mathrm{odd}}}b_{ij}^{w}\left(\prod_{S\in X}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{S}}\prod_{ij\in S:i<j}e^{-\beta w_{ij}}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{A}_{ij}}\right)\prod_{i\in U_{0}}(-\mathbf{1}_{|x_{i}-y_{i}|\geq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}})\\
\times\prod_{ij\in\cup_{l}\mathcal{E}^{\mathrm{inter}}(X_{l})}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{ij}}\prod_{ij\in F}(-\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{ij}})\prod_{i\in V_{X}}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(x_{i}-y_{i})}\mathrm{d}x_{i}\mathrm{d}y_{i}.

Step 2: bounding
J_{2}

Let
(Y_{1},\ldots,Y_{m})
be the connected components (relative to
X
), with at least two multipoles, of the graph
(V_{X},E^{\mathrm{odd}})
. Let
T^{b}\in\mathsf{T}^{\mathrm{Coa}_{X}(Y_{1},\ldots,Y_{m})}
be such that
T^{b}\subset E^{\mathrm{even}}\cup F
.

For every
l\in[m]
, let
E_{1,l}^{\prime}
be the subset of edges in
E^{\mathrm{odd}}
adjacent to some vertex in
V_{Y_{l}}
and let
E_{1,l}^{\prime\prime}
be a minimal
2
-edge connected (relative to
X
) subgraph of
(V_{Y_{l}},E_{1,l}^{\prime})
. Set
E_{1}^{\prime\prime}=\cup_{l\in[m]}E_{1,l}^{\prime\prime}
.

Recall that there exists
C>0
depending on
\beta
such that

(A.58)

\displaystyle|a_{ij}^{v}|

\displaystyle\leq Ca^{\mathrm{abs}}_{ij},\quad|a_{ij}^{w}-a_{ij}^{v}|\leq Ca^{\mathrm{abs}}_{ij},

\displaystyle|b_{ij}^{v}|

\displaystyle\leq Cb^{\mathrm{abs}}_{ij},\quad|b_{ij}^{w}-b_{ij}^{v}|\leq Cb^{\mathrm{abs}}_{ij}

\bullet
Suppose that
U_{1}\neq\emptyset
, or
U_{2}\neq\emptyset
, or
F_{3}\neq\emptyset
(case A). Using the last display and proceeding as in the proof of Lemma
A.6
, see (
A.40
), we get that there exists
C>0
depending on
\beta
and
k
such that

(A.59)

|J_{2}(F_{1},F_{2},F_{3},U_{1},U_{2})|\leq C\max_{i_{0}\in V_{X}}\int_{(\Lambda^{2})^{|V_{X}|}}\left(\frac{\max_{i\in V_{X}}r_{i}^{2}}{\tau_{i_{0}}^{2}}+\mathbf{1}_{i_{0}\notin I_{\mathrm{good}}^{Z}}\right)\prod_{ij\in E_{1}^{\prime\prime}}a^{\mathrm{abs}}_{ij}\prod_{ij\in T^{b}}b^{\mathrm{abs}}_{ij}\prod_{S\in X}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{S}}\\
\times\prod_{i\in V_{X}}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(x_{i}-y_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{|x_{i}-y_{i}|\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}x_{i}\mathrm{d}y_{i}.

\bullet
Suppose that
E_{1}\setminus E_{1}^{\prime\prime}
intersects
F_{1}
(case B) and let
i_{0}j_{0}
be in this intersection. Using (
A.39
) we have

(A.60)

|a_{i_{0}j_{0}}^{w}-a_{i_{0}j_{0}}^{v}|\leq C|w_{i_{0}j_{0}}-v_{i_{0}j_{0}}|\leq C\frac{r_{i_{0}}r_{j_{0}}}{\min(\tau_{i_{0}},\tau_{j_{0}})^{2}}\leq\frac{\max_{i\in V_{X}}r_{i}^{2}}{\min(\tau_{i_{0}},\tau_{j_{0}})^{2}},

Therefore, using (
A.58
), there exists
C>0
depending on
\beta
and
k
such that

(A.61)

|J_{2}(F_{1},F_{2},F_{3},U_{1},U_{2})|\leq C\max_{i_{0}\in V_{X}}\int_{(\Lambda^{2})^{|V_{X}|}}\frac{\max_{i\in V_{X}}r_{i}^{2}}{\tau_{i_{0}}^{2}}\prod_{ij\in E_{1}^{\prime\prime}}a^{\mathrm{abs}}_{ij}\prod_{ij\in T^{b}}b^{\mathrm{abs}}_{ij}\prod_{S\in X}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{S}}\\
\times\prod_{i\in V_{X}}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(x_{i}-y_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{|x_{i}-y_{i}|\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}x_{i}\mathrm{d}y_{i}.

\bullet
Suppose that
E^{\mathrm{even}}\setminus T^{b}
intersects
F_{2}
(case C) and let
i_{0}j_{0}
be in this intersection. We use the crude bound, again thanks to (
A.39
),

|b_{i_{0}j_{0}}^{w}-b_{i_{0}j_{0}}^{v}|\leq C|w_{i_{0}j_{0}}-v_{i_{0}j_{0}}|\leq C\frac{\max_{i\in V_{X}}r_{i}^{2}}{\min(\tau_{i_{0}},\tau_{j_{0}})^{2}}

and conclude as above that

(A.62)

|J_{2}(F_{1},F_{2},F_{3},U_{1},U_{2})|\leq C\max_{i_{0}\in V_{X}}\int_{(\Lambda^{2})^{|V_{X}|}}\frac{\max_{i\in V_{X}}r_{i}^{2}}{\tau_{i_{0}}^{2}}\prod_{ij\in E_{1}^{\prime\prime}}a^{\mathrm{abs}}_{ij}\prod_{ij\in T^{b}}b^{\mathrm{abs}}_{ij}\prod_{S\in X}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{S}}\\
\times\prod_{i\in V_{X}}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(x_{i}-y_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{|x_{i}-y_{i}|\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}x_{i}\mathrm{d}y_{i}.

\bullet
Suppose that there exists
l_{0}\in[m]
such that
E_{1,l_{0}}^{\prime\prime}\cap F_{1}\neq\emptyset
(case D) and let
i_{0}j_{0}\in E_{1,l_{0}}^{\prime\prime}\cap F_{1}
. Then, we select a strict ear decomposition
(P_{1},\ldots,P_{K})
of
(V_{Y_{l_{0}}},E_{1,l_{0}}^{\prime\prime})
as in Definition
4.35
such that the base cycle
P_{1}
contains the edge
i_{0}j_{0}
(which is possible by Lemma
4.36
). We perform a peeling of the ears as in Definition
4.39
except that the base cycle is opened at
i_{0}j_{0}
: instead of (
4.64
), we set
\vec{S}(P_{1})=i_{0}\to j_{0}
(where the orientation is arbitrary). We let
T_{l_{0}}^{a}
be the resulting tree. Let us emphasize that
i_{0}j_{0}\notin T_{l_{0}}^{a}
. Since the opening of the ears distinct from the cycle is as in Definition
4.39
, we get from Lemma
4.41
that

(A.63)

\prod_{ij\in P_{2}\cup\cdots\cup P_{K}}a^{\mathrm{abs}}_{ij}\leq C\prod_{S\in Y_{l_{0}}\setminus\mathrm{Res}(Y_{l_{0}},P_{1})}r_{S}^{2}\max_{T\in B(\cdot,T_{l_{0}}^{a})}\prod_{ij\in T\setminus P_{1}}\frac{1}{d_{ij}^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{d_{ij}\leq 16\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{ij}^{c}}.

It remains to consider the opening of the base cycle. Clearly,

|a_{i_{0}j_{0}}^{w}-a_{i_{0}j_{0}}^{v}|\prod_{ij\in P_{1}\setminus\{i_{0}j_{0}\}}a_{ij}^{\mathrm{abs}}\leq C|a_{i_{0}j_{0}}^{w}-a_{i_{0}j_{0}}^{v}|r_{i_{0}}r_{j_{0}}\prod_{S\in\mathrm{Res}(Y_{l_{0}},P_{1}):S\neq[i_{0}]^{X},[j_{0}]^{X}}r_{S}^{2}\\
\times\max_{T\in B(\cdot,T_{l_{0}}^{a})}\prod_{ij\in T\cap P_{1}}\frac{1}{d_{ij}^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{d_{ij}\leq 16\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{ij}^{c}}.

Inserting (
A.60
), we get

|a_{i_{0}j_{0}}^{w}-a_{i_{0}j_{0}}^{v}|\prod_{ij\in P_{1}\setminus\{i_{0}j_{0}\}}a^{\mathrm{abs}}_{ij}\leq\frac{C}{\min(\tau_{i_{0}},\tau_{j_{0}})^{2}}\prod_{S\in\mathrm{Res}(Y_{l_{0}},P_{1})}r_{S}^{2}\max_{T\in B(\cdot,T_{l_{0}}^{a})}\prod_{ij\in T\cap P_{1}}\frac{1}{d_{ij}^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{d_{ij}\leq 16\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{ij}^{c}}.

Combining this with (
A.63
) gives

(A.64)

|a_{i_{0}j_{0}}^{w}-a_{i_{0}j_{0}}^{v}|\prod_{ij\in E_{1,{l_{0}}}^{\prime\prime}\setminus\{i_{0}j_{0}\}}a_{ij}^{\mathrm{abs}}\leq\frac{C}{\min(\tau_{i_{0}},\tau_{j_{0}})^{2}}\prod_{S\in Y_{l_{0}}}r_{S}^{2}\max_{T\in B(\cdot,T_{l_{0}}^{a})}\prod_{ij\in T}\frac{1}{d_{ij}^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{d_{ij}\leq 16\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{ij}^{c}}.

For
l\in[m]\setminus\{l_{0}\}
, we let
T_{l}^{a}\coloneqq\mathcal{T}^{X_{l}}(\cdot,E_{1,l}^{\prime\prime})
be as in Definition
4.40
. Set
T^{a}\coloneqq\cup_{l=1}^{n}T_{l}^{a}
. By Corollary
4.43
and (
A.64
),

|a_{i_{0}j_{0}}^{w}-a_{i_{0}j_{0}}^{v}|\prod_{l=1}^{n}\prod_{ij\in E_{1,l}^{\prime\prime}\setminus\{i_{0}j_{0}\}}a_{ij}^{\mathrm{abs}}\leq\frac{Cr_{\hat{S}_{l_{0}}^{2}}}{\min(\tau_{i_{0}},\tau_{j_{0}})^{2}}\prod_{S\in\cup_{l}(X_{l}\setminus\hat{S}_{l})}r_{S}^{2}\max_{T\in B(\cdot,T^{a})}\prod_{ij\in T}\frac{1}{d_{ij}^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{d_{ij}\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{ij}^{c}}.

We conclude that in this case

|J_{2}(F_{1},F_{2},F_{3},U_{1},U_{2})|\leq C\max_{i_{0}\in V_{X}}\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}T_{1}^{a}\in\mathsf{T}^{X_{1}}\\
\ldots\\
T_{n}^{a}\in\mathsf{T}^{X_{n}}\end{subarray}}\int_{(\Lambda^{2})^{|V_{X}|}}\frac{\max_{i\in V_{X}}r_{i}^{2}}{\tau_{i_{0}}^{2}}\prod_{S\in\cup_{l}(X_{l}\setminus\hat{S}_{l})}r_{S}^{2}\prod_{ij\in\cup_{l}T_{l}^{a}}\frac{1}{d_{ij}^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{d_{ij}\leq 16\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{ij}^{c}}\\
\times\prod_{ij\in T^{b}}b_{ij}^{\mathrm{abs}}\prod_{S\in X}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{S}}\prod_{i\in V_{X}}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(x_{i}-y_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{|x_{i}-y_{i}|\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}x_{i}\mathrm{d}y_{i},

\bullet
Suppose that
T^{b}\cap F_{2}\neq\emptyset
(case E) and let
i_{0}j_{0}\in T^{b}\cap F_{2}
.
Using (
A.58
), we have

|b_{i_{0}j_{0}}^{w}-b_{i_{0}j_{0}}^{v}|\leq C|w_{i_{0}j_{0}}-v_{i_{0}j_{0}}||v_{i_{0}j_{0}}|.

Using Lemma
3.9
or
3.10
, we find that on the event
\mathcal{B}_{S}
,

(A.65)

|b_{i_{0}j_{0}}^{w}-b_{i_{0}j_{0}}^{v}|\leq C\frac{r_{i_{0}}^{2}r_{j_{0}}^{2}}{\max(\tau_{i_{0}},\tau_{j_{0}},d_{i_{0}j_{0}})^{2}d_{i_{0}j_{0}}\max(d_{i_{0}j_{0}},r_{i_{0}},r_{j_{0}})}\leq C\frac{\max_{i\in V_{X}}r_{i}^{2}}{\max(\tau_{i_{0}},\tau_{j_{0}})^{2}}a_{i_{0}j_{0}}^{\mathrm{abs}}.

Therefore,

(A.66)

|J_{2}(F_{1},F_{2},F_{3},U_{1},U_{2})|\leq C\int_{(\Lambda^{2})^{|V_{X}|}}\frac{\max_{i\in V_{X}}r_{i}^{2}}{\max(\tau_{i_{0}},\tau_{j_{0}})^{2}}\\
\times\prod_{ij\in E_{1}^{\prime\prime}\cup\{i_{0}j_{0}\}}a_{ij}^{\mathrm{abs}}\prod_{ij\in T^{b}\setminus\{i_{0}j_{0}\}}b_{ij}^{\mathrm{abs}}\prod_{S\in X}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{S}}\prod_{i\in V_{X}}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(x_{i}-y_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{|x_{i}-y_{i}|\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}x_{i}\mathrm{d}y_{i}.

\bullet
We now consider the term (
A.57
) (case F). Let
i_{0}\in U_{0}
. By Corollary
4.43
, we have

(A.67)

|J(E_{1},U_{0})|\leq C\max_{\tilde{T}^{a}}\int_{(\Lambda^{2})^{|V_{X}|}}\min\Bigr(\frac{\max_{i\in V_{X}}r_{i}}{\max_{e\in\tilde{T}^{a}}d_{e}},1\Bigr)^{2}\prod_{S\in\cup_{l}X_{l}}r_{S}^{2}\prod_{ij\in\tilde{T}^{a}}\frac{1}{d_{ij}^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{d_{ij}\leq 16\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{ij}^{c}}\\
\times\prod_{ij\in T^{b}}b_{ij}^{\mathrm{abs}}\prod_{S\in X}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{S}}\left(\prod_{ij\in S:i<j}e^{-\beta w_{ij}}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{A}_{ij}}\right)\prod_{i\in U_{0}}\mathbf{1}_{|x_{i}-y_{i}|\geq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\prod_{i\in V_{X}}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(x_{i}-y_{i})}\mathrm{d}x_{i}\mathrm{d}y_{i}.

Step 3: integration in cases A, B, C, D, E

By (
A.59
), (
A.61
), (
A.62
) and Lemma
3.7
, there exists
C>0
depending on
\beta,M
and
k
such that

|J_{2}(F_{1},F_{2},F_{3},U_{1},U_{2})|\leq C\max_{i_{0}\in V_{X}}\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}T_{1}^{a}\in\mathsf{T}^{X_{1}}\\
\ldots\\
T_{n}^{a}\in\mathsf{T}^{X_{n}}\end{subarray}}\int_{(\Lambda^{2})^{|V_{X}|}}\left(\frac{\max_{i\in V_{X}}r_{i}^{2}}{\tau_{i_{0}}^{2}}+\mathbf{1}_{i_{0}\notin I_{\mathrm{good}}^{Z}}\right)\prod_{S\in\cup_{l}(X_{l}\setminus\{\hat{S}_{l})\}}r_{S}^{2}\\
\times\prod_{ij\in T^{a}}\frac{1}{d_{ij}^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{d_{ij}\leq 16\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{ij}^{c}}\prod_{i\in V_{X}}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(x_{i}-y_{i})}\mathrm{d}x_{i}\mathrm{d}y_{i},

where for every
l\in[n]
,
\hat{S}_{l}
stands for the index
S
of the largest
r_{S}
,
S\in X_{l}
. Splitting the integral and changing variables as in the proof of Lemma
A.6
, we obtain that there exists
C>0
depending on
\beta,M
and
k
such that

|J_{2}(F_{1},F_{2},F_{3},U_{1},U_{2})|\leq C\max_{i_{0}\in V_{X}}\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}T_{1}^{a}\in\mathsf{T}^{X_{1}}\\
\ldots\\
T_{n}^{a}\in\mathsf{T}^{X_{n}}\end{subarray}}\int\left(\frac{r_{i_{0}}^{2}}{\tau_{i_{0}}^{2}}+\mathbf{1}_{i_{0}\notin I_{\mathrm{good}}^{Z}}\right)\Bigr(\log\frac{16\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}{\min_{i\in V_{X}}r_{i}}\Bigr)^{k}\frac{\prod_{i\in V_{X}}r_{i}^{2}}{\max_{i\in V_{X}}r_{i}^{2}}\\
\times\prod_{i\in V_{X}}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(r_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{r_{i}\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\prod_{i\in V_{X}}\mathrm{d}\vec{r}_{i}\mathrm{d}x_{i_{0}}.

Recall that if
i_{0}\notin I_{\mathrm{good}}^{Z}
, then (
A.44
) holds.
Hence, proceeding as in the proof of Lemma
A.6
, we obtain that there exists
C>0
depending on
\beta,M,\varepsilon_{0}
and
k
such that

\int\mathbf{1}_{i_{0}\notin I_{\mathrm{good}}^{Z}}\Bigr(\log\frac{16\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}{\min_{i\in V_{X}}r_{i}}\Bigr)^{k}\frac{\prod_{i\in V_{X}}r_{i}^{2}}{\max_{i\in V_{X}}r_{i}^{2}}\\
\times\prod_{i\in V_{X}}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(r_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{r_{i}\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\prod_{i\in V_{X}}\mathrm{d}\vec{r}_{i}\mathrm{d}x_{i_{0}}\leq C(|V_{\mathrm{bad}}|+N\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}^{-2})\lambda^{(2-\beta)k}.

Moreover, arguing as in the proof of Lemma
A.6
, there exists
C>0
depending on
\beta,M,\varepsilon_{0}
and
k
such that

\max_{i_{0}\in S}\int\frac{1}{\tau_{i_{0}}^{2}}\Bigr(\log\frac{16\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}{\min_{i\in V_{X}}r_{i}}\Bigr)^{k}\prod_{i\in V_{X}}\Bigr(r_{i}^{2}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(r_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{r_{i}\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\Bigr)\prod_{i\in V_{X}}\mathrm{d}\vec{r}_{i}\mathrm{d}x_{i_{0}}\\
\leq C(|V_{\mathrm{bad}}|+N\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}^{-2})\lambda^{(2-\beta)k}.

Combining these two displays, we deduce that there exists
C>0
depending on
\beta,M,\varepsilon_{0}
and
k
such that

(A.68)

|J_{2}(F_{1},F_{2},F_{3},U_{1},U_{2})|\leq C(|V_{\mathrm{bad}}|+N\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}^{-2})\lambda^{(2-\beta)k}.

Step 4: integration in case F

Using (
A.67
) and (
A.50
), there exists
C>0
depending on
\beta,M,\varepsilon_{0}
and
k
such that

(A.69)

|J(E_{1},U_{0})|\leq CN\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}^{-2}\lambda^{(2-\beta)k}.

Step 5: conclusion

Combining (
A.68
) and (
A.55
), there exists
C>0
depending on
\beta,M,\varepsilon_{0}
and
k
such that

|I_{2}^{w,\tau,L,\varepsilon_{0},Z}-I_{2}^{v,\infty,0,\varepsilon_{0},\emptyset}|\leq C(|V_{\mathrm{bad}}|+N\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}^{-2})\lambda^{(2-\beta)k}.

Inserting (
A.69
) into (
A.56
) also gives the existence of
C>0
depending on
\beta,M,\varepsilon_{0}
and
k
such that

|I_{2}^{v,\infty,0,\varepsilon_{0},\emptyset}-I_{2}^{v,\infty,0,\infty,\emptyset}|\leq CN\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}^{-2}\lambda^{(2-\beta)k},

which concludes the proof.
∎

Lemma A.8
.

Let
\beta\in(2,\infty)
. Let
\varepsilon_{0}\in(0,1)
. Let
V\subset[N]
be such that
|V|\leq p^{*}(\beta)
.
Define

I_{3}^{\varepsilon_{0}}(V)\coloneqq\sum_{E\in\mathcal{G}_{c}(V)}\int\prod_{ij\in E}f_{ij}^{v}\prod_{i\in V}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(x_{i}-y_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{|x_{i}-y_{i}|\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}x_{i}\mathrm{d}y_{i}.

Then, there exists
C>0
depending on
\beta
,
|V|
and
\varepsilon_{0}
such that

|I_{3}^{\varepsilon_{0}}(V)-I_{3}^{\infty}(V)|\leq CN\lambda^{(2-\beta)|V|}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}^{-2}.

The proof of Lemma
A.8
is similar to the proof of Lemma
A.7
and is therefore omitted.

A.5.
Integral for unbounded clusters

For the proof of Lemma
4.53
, we will need the following summation result on trees.

Lemma A.9
.

Let
S=\{1,\ldots,p\}
. Let
(x_{i})_{i\in S}\in\mathbb{R}^{|S|}
. We have

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}T\mathrm{connected}\\
\mathrm{tree}\,\mathrm{on}\,S\end{subarray}}\prod_{i\in S}x_{i}^{\deg_{T}(i)}=|S|^{|S|-2}\Bigr(\prod_{i\in S}x_{i}\Bigr)\Bigr(\frac{1}{|S|}\sum_{i\in S}x_{i}\Bigr)^{|S|-2}.

Proof.

For any
T
tree on
S
we have
\sum_{i\in S}\deg_{T}(i)=2(|S|-1)
.
Let
\mathcal{D}
be the set of sequences
(d_{i})_{i\in S}
such that
\sum_{i\in S}d_{i}=2(|S|-1)
and for every
i\in S
,
d_{i}\in\mathbb{N}^{*}
. One may write

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}T\mathrm{connected}\\
\mathrm{tree}\,\mathrm{on}\,S\end{subarray}}\prod_{i\in S}x_{i}^{\deg_{T}(i)}=\sum_{d\in\mathcal{D}}\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}(d)}\prod_{i\in S}x_{i}^{d_{i}},

where for every
\vec{d}\in\mathcal{D}
,
\mathcal{T}(\vec{d})
stands for the set of trees on
S
with degree sequence
\vec{d}
. By Prüfer’s bijection
[
Prü18
]
, for every
\vec{d}\in\mathcal{D}
, we have

|\mathcal{T}(\vec{d})|=\frac{(|S|-2)!}{\prod_{i\in S}(d_{i}-1)!}.

Therefore,

\sum_{T\in\mathcal{T}(d)}\prod_{i\in S}x_{i}^{\deg_{T}(i)}=\frac{(|S|-2)!}{\prod_{i\in S}(d_{i}-1)!}\prod_{i\in S}x_{i}^{d_{i}}.

Let us now sum this over
d\in\mathcal{D}
. By the change of variables
d_{i}^{\prime}=d_{i}-1
, one can write

\sum_{d\in\mathcal{D}}\prod_{i\in S}\frac{x_{i}^{d_{i}}}{(d_{i}-1)!}=\sum_{d\in\mathcal{D}^{\prime}}\prod_{i\in S}\frac{x_{i}^{d_{i}+1}}{d_{i}!}=\prod_{i\in S}x_{i}\sum_{d\in\mathcal{D}^{\prime}}\prod_{i\in S}\frac{x_{i}^{d_{i}}}{d_{i}!},

where
\mathcal{D}^{\prime}
is the set of sequences
(d_{i})_{i\in S}
such that
\sum_{i\in S}d_{i}=|S|-2
and for every
i\in S
,
d_{i}\in\mathbb{N}
. One can notice that

\sum_{d\in\mathcal{D}^{\prime}}\prod_{i\in S}\frac{x_{i}^{d_{i}}}{d_{i}!}=\frac{1}{(|S|-2)!}\Bigr(\sum_{i\in S}x_{i}\Bigr)^{|S|-2}.

Indeed, the right-hand side of the last display can be viewed as the coefficient of
t^{|S|-2}
in the expansion of
\prod_{i\in S}e^{x_{i}t}
. Combining the last relations concludes the proof.
∎

Proof of Lemma
4.53
.

Denote
Y\coloneqq\mathrm{Coa}_{X}(X_{1},\ldots,X_{n})
.

Recall (
4.114
). Using
\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{S}}\leq\sum_{T_{S}\in\mathcal{T}_{c}(S)}\prod_{ij\in T_{S}}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{ij}}
and Cayley’s theorem, we obtain

\mathcal{J}_{C_{0}}(\cup_{l=1}^{n}T_{l}^{a},T^{b})\leq\Bigr(\prod_{S\in X}|S|^{|S|-2}\max_{T_{S}\in\mathcal{T}_{c}(S)}\Bigr)I(\cup_{l=1}^{n}T_{l}^{a},T^{b},(T_{S})),

where

I(\cup_{l=1}^{n}T_{l}^{a},T^{b},(T_{S}))\coloneqq\int_{(\Lambda^{2})^{|V_{X}|}}\prod_{ij\in T^{b}}b_{ij}^{\mathrm{abs}}\prod_{l=1}^{n}\Bigr(\prod_{S\in X_{l},S\neq\hat{S}_{l}}r_{S}^{2}\Bigr)\prod_{ij\in\cup_{l=1}^{n}T_{l}^{a}}\Bigr(\frac{1}{d_{ij}^{2}}\mathbf{1}_{d_{ij}\leq 16\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{ij}^{c}}\Bigr)\\
\times\prod_{S\in X}\prod_{ij\in T_{S}}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{B}_{ij}}\prod_{i\in V_{X}}\Bigr(\Bigr(\frac{\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}{r_{i}}\Bigr)^{\frac{C_{0}}{M}}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(x_{i}-y_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{|x_{i}-y_{i}|\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}x_{i}\mathrm{d}y_{i}\Bigr).

Splitting the phase space according to the variables that attain
d_{ij}
, for every
ij\in\cup_{S}T_{S}\cup T^{b}\cup T^{a}
as in (
A.9
), using the variables (
A.10
) and using Lemma
A.1
, recalling that
d_{ij}\geq M\min(r_{i},r_{j})
in
\mathcal{B}_{ij}^{c}
, we get that there exists
C>0
depending on
\beta
and
M
such that

I(\cup_{l=1}^{n}T_{l}^{a},T^{b},(T_{S}))\leq NC^{k}\int\prod_{ij\in T^{b}\cup_{S\in X}T_{S}}\max(\min(r_{i},r_{j}),\lambda)^{2}\prod_{l=1}^{n}\Bigr(\prod_{S\in X_{l},S\neq\hat{S}_{l}}r_{S}^{2}\Bigr)\\
\times\prod_{ij\in T^{a}\cup T^{b}}\log\frac{16\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}{\min(r_{i},r_{j})}\prod_{i\in V_{X}}\Bigr(\frac{\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}{r_{i}}\Bigr)^{\frac{C_{0}}{M}}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(r_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{r_{i}\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}\vec{r}_{i}.

Notice that

\begin{split}\prod_{ij\in T^{a}_{l}}\log\frac{16\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}{\min(r_{i},r_{j})}&\leq C^{k}\prod_{i\in V_{X_{l}}}\Bigr(\log\frac{16\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}{r_{i}}\Bigr)^{\deg_{T^{a}_{l}}(i)}\\
&\leq C^{k}\prod_{S\in X_{l}}\Bigr(\log\frac{16\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}{\min_{i\in S}r_{i}}\Bigr)^{p(\beta)\deg_{(V_{X},T^{a}_{l})/X}(S)}.\end{split}

Therefore, applying Lemma
A.9
to the quotient tree
(V_{X_{l}},T^{a})/X
and to the numbers

x_{S}\coloneqq\Bigr(\log\frac{16\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}{\min_{i\in S}r_{i}}\Bigr)^{p(\beta)},\quad S\in X_{l},

we get that there exists
C>0
depending on
p(\beta)
such that

\sum_{T_{l}^{a}\in\mathsf{T}^{X_{l}}}\prod_{ij\in T_{l}^{a}}\log\frac{16\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}{\min(r_{i},r_{j})}\leq C^{k}|X|^{|X|}\left(\frac{1}{|X_{l}|}\sum_{S\in X_{l}}\Bigr(\log\frac{16\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}{\min_{i\in S}r_{i}}\Bigr)^{p(\beta)}\right)^{|X_{l}|-2}\prod_{i\in V_{X_{l}}}\Bigr(\log\frac{16\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}{r_{i}}\Bigr).

Therefore, there exists a constant
C>0
depending on
p(\beta)
and
M
such that

(A.70)

\sum_{T_{l}^{a}\in\mathsf{T}^{X_{l}}}\prod_{ij\in T_{l}^{a}}\log\frac{16\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}{\min(r_{i},r_{j})}\leq C^{k}|X_{l}|^{|X_{l}|}\prod_{i\in V_{X_{l}}}\Bigr(\frac{16\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}{r_{i}}\Bigr)^{\frac{C_{0}}{M}}.

Similarly,

(A.71)

\sum_{T^{b}\in\mathsf{T}^{Y}}\prod_{ij\in T^{b}}\log\frac{16\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}{\min(r_{i},r_{j})}\leq C^{k}|Y|^{|Y|}\prod_{i\in V_{X}}\Bigr(\frac{16\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}{r_{i}}\Bigr)^{\frac{C_{0}}{M}}.

By (
A.26
),

(A.72)

\prod_{l=1}^{n}\prod_{S\in X_{l}:S\neq\hat{S}_{l}}r_{S}^{2}\prod_{ij\in T^{b}}\max(\min(r_{i},r_{j}),\lambda)^{2}\prod_{ij\in\cup_{S\in X}R_{S}}\max(\min(r_{i},r_{j}),\lambda)^{2}\leq\frac{\prod_{i\in V_{X}}\max(r_{i},\lambda)^{2}}{\max_{i\in V_{X}}\max(r_{i},\lambda)^{2}}.

Therefore, combining (
A.70
), (
A.71
) and (
A.72
), we obtain that there exists
C>0
depending on
\beta,p(\beta)
and
M
such that

(A.73)

\Bigr(\prod_{l=1}^{n}\sum_{T_{l}^{a}\in\mathsf{T}^{X_{l}}}\Bigr)\sum_{T^{b}\in\mathsf{T}^{Y}}I(\cup_{l=1}^{n}T_{l}^{a},T^{b},(T_{S}))\\
\leq NC^{k}\prod_{l=1}^{n}|X_{l}|^{|X_{l}|}|Y|^{|Y|}\int\frac{\prod_{i\in V_{X}}\max(r_{i},\lambda)^{2}}{\max_{i\in V_{X}}\max(r_{i},\lambda)^{2}}\prod_{i\in V_{X}}\Bigr(\frac{\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}{r_{i}}\Bigr)^{\frac{2C_{0}}{M}}e^{\beta\mathsf{g}_{\lambda}(r_{i})}\mathbf{1}_{r_{i}\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}\mathrm{d}\vec{r}_{i}.

\bullet
Suppose that
\beta\in(2,4)
. Taking
M
large enough that
4-\beta-\frac{2C_{0}}{M}\geq\frac{4-\beta}{2}
and isolating the largest
r_{i}
, we get from (
A.73
) that there exists
C>0
depending on
\beta,p(\beta)
and
M
such that

\Bigr(\prod_{l=1}^{n}\sum_{T_{l}^{a}\in\mathsf{T}^{X_{l}}}\Bigr)\sum_{T^{b}\in\mathsf{T}^{Y}}I(\cup_{l=1}^{n}T_{l}^{a},T^{b},(T_{S}))\leq NC^{k}\prod_{l=1}^{n}|X_{l}|^{|X_{l}|}|Y|^{|Y|}\int_{0}^{\infty}r^{(4-\beta)k-3}\Bigr(\frac{\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}{r}\Bigr)^{\frac{2C_{0}}{M}k}\mathbf{1}_{r\leq\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}.

By choice of
M
,

(4-\beta)k-3-\frac{2C_{0}}{M}\geq\Bigr(\frac{4-\beta}{2}\Bigr)k-3>-1,

since by assumption of the lemma,
k>\frac{4}{4-\beta}
. Therefore, there exists
C>0
depending on
\beta
and
M
such that

\Bigr(\prod_{l=1}^{n}\sum_{T_{l}^{a}\in\mathsf{T}^{X_{l}}}\Bigr)\sum_{T^{b}\in\mathsf{T}^{Y}}I(\cup_{l=1}^{n}T_{l}^{a},T^{b},(T_{S}))\leq NC^{k}(\varepsilon_{0}R_{\beta,\lambda})^{(4-\beta)k-2}.

Recalling that
R_{\beta,\lambda}^{4-\beta}=\lambda^{2-\beta}
, this concludes the proof in the case
\beta\in(2,4)
.

\bullet
Suppose that
\beta\geq 4
. Then, there exists
C>0
depending on
\beta,p(\beta)
and
M
such that

(A.74)

\Bigr(\prod_{l=1}^{n}\sum_{T_{l}^{a}\in\mathsf{T}^{X_{l}}}\Bigr)\sum_{T^{b}\in\mathsf{T}^{Y}}I(\cup_{l=1}^{n}T_{l}^{a},T^{b},(T_{S}))\leq NC^{k}\prod_{l=1}^{n}|X_{l}|^{|X_{l}|}|Y|^{|Y|}\\
\times\Bigr(\frac{\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}{\lambda}\Bigr)^{\frac{2C_{0}}{M}k}\Bigr(\lambda^{(4-\beta)k-2}\mathbf{1}_{\beta>4}+\lambda^{-2}|\log\lambda|\mathbf{1}_{\beta=4}\Bigr).

Notice that

\Bigr(\lambda^{(4-\beta)k-2}\mathbf{1}_{\beta>4}+\lambda^{-2}|\log\lambda|\mathbf{1}_{\beta=4}\Bigr)\Bigr(\frac{\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}{\lambda}\Bigr)^{\frac{2C_{0}}{M}k}=\lambda^{(2-\beta)k}\lambda^{2(k-1)}(\varepsilon_{0}\lambda^{-2p_{0}-1})^{\frac{2C_{0}}{M}k}(\mathbf{1}_{\beta>4}+|\log\lambda|\mathbf{1}_{\beta=4}).

Since
k>2p_{0}
, taking
M
large enough with respect to
p_{0}
and
\lambda
small enough, we deduce that

\Bigr(\frac{\varepsilon_{0}\bar{R}_{\beta,\lambda}}{\lambda}\Bigr)^{\frac{2C_{0}}{M}k}\Bigr(\lambda^{(4-\beta)k-2}\mathbf{1}_{\beta>4}+\lambda^{-2}|\log\lambda|\mathbf{1}_{\beta=4}\Bigr)\leq\varepsilon_{0}^{2\alpha(\beta)k-2}\lambda^{(2-\beta)k}\lambda^{2p_{0}},

when
\lambda
is small enough with respect to
\varepsilon_{0}
.
Inserting this into (
A.74
) concludes the proof in the case
\beta\geq 4
.

∎
